Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs

AddSubgroupstructureMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, type
Wording
additive subgroup of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then the additive subgroups of $G$ are a basic type.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors les sous-groupes additifs de $G$ sont un type de base.
AddSubgroup.NormalclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, predicate
Wording
H is normal (from the name)
English
Let $H$ be an additive subgroup of $A$. Then we can say that $H$ is normal.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (A)\vdash \operatorname{Normal} (H)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-groupe additif de $A$. Alors nous pouvons dire que $H$ est normal.
AddSubgroup.copydefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the copy of K and s (from the name)
English
Let $K$ be an additive subgroup of $G$. Let $s$ be an element of $\wp G$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {s}{K}$. Then the copy of $K$ and $s$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$K \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G), s \in \wp G , \EqtMACRoJ {s}{K}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (K , s , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $K$ un sous-groupe additif de $G$. Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp G$ tel que $\EqtMACRoH {s}{K}$. Alors le copy de $K$ et $s$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.inclusiondefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the inclusion homomorphism of h (from the docstring)
English
Assume that $H \leq K$. Then the inclusion homomorphism of $h$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {K}{x_{2}})}$.
Formula
$H \leq K \vdash \operatorname{inclusion} (h)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {H}{x_{1}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {K}{x_{2}})})$
French
Supposons que $H \leq K$. Alors l'homomorphisme d'inclusions de $h$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {K}{x_{2}})}$.
AddSubgroup.normalizerdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the normalizer of S (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be an element of $\wp G$. Then the normalizer of $S$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$S \in \wp G \vdash \operatorname{normalizer} (S)\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $S$ un élément de $\wp G$. Alors le normalisateur de $S$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.ofClassdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive subgroup of s (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an element of $S$. Then the additive subgroup of $s$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$s \in S \vdash \operatorname{ofClass} (s)\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $s$ un élément de $S$. Alors le sous-groupe additif de $s$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.ofSubdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive subgroup of s (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an element of $\wp G$ such that $s$ is nonempty and for all elements $x_{1}$ of $G$, if $x_{1}$ is an element of $s$, then for all elements $y$ of $G$, if $y$ is an element of $s$, then $x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)$ is an element of $s$. Then the additive subgroup of $s$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$s \in \wp G , \operatorname{Nonempty} (s), \forall x_{1} \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{1}}\rightarrow (\forall y \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{y}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)})\vdash \operatorname{ofSub} (s , \mathrm{hsn} , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp G$ tel que $s$ soit nonempty et pour tous des éléments $x_{1}$ de $G$, si $x_{1}$ est un élément de $s$, alors pour tous des éléments $y$ de $G$, si $y$ est un élément de $s$, alors $x_{1} + \operatorname{neg} (y)$ soit un élément de $s$. Alors le sous-groupe additif de $s$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.
AddSubgroup.subtypedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the subtype of H (from the name)
English
Let $H$ be an additive subgroup of $G$. Then the subtype of $H$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})}$ and $G$.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)\vdash \operatorname{subtype} (H)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {H}{x_{1}})}, G)$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-groupe additif de $G$. Alors le subtype de $H$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})}$ et $G$.
AddSubgroupClassclassMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Defs, predicate
Wording
G is a additive subgroup class (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a set. Let $G$ be an element of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of sets. Then we can say that $G$ is an additive subgroup class.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Set} , G \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{Set})\vdash \mathrm{AddSubgroupClass} (S , G)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $S$ un ensemble. Soit $G$ un élément de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles. Alors nous pouvons dire que $G$ est une classe de sous-groupes additive.

AddSubsemigroup.evendefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Even, function or constant
Wording
the even of S (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a set. Then the even of $S$ is an additive subsemigroup of $S$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $s$ to the proposition that $s$ is even and the function that maps $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ to $\operatorname{add}$.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{even} (S):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda s . \mathrm{even} (s)), \lambda x_{1} , x_{2} . \operatorname{add})\in \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (S)$
French
Soit $S$ un ensemble. Alors le pair de $S$ est un sous-demi-groupe additif de $S$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $s$ à la proposition que $s$ est pair et la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ à $\operatorname{add}$.

Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker

AddMonoidHom.eqLocusdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ and $g$ be additive monoid homomorphisms of $G$ and $M$. Then $\operatorname{eqLocus}$ applied to $f$ and $g$ is an additive subgroup of $G$.
Formula
$f , g \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (G , M)\vdash \operatorname{eqLocus} (f , g)\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soient $f$ et $g$ des homomorphismes de monoïdes additifs de $G$ et $M$. Alors $\operatorname{eqLocus}$ appliqué à $f$ et $g$ est un sous-groupe additif de $G$.

AddMonoidHom.ofInjectivedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker, function or constant
Wording
the additive monoid homomorphism of hf (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$ is injective. Then the additive monoid homomorphism of $\mathrm{hf}$ is an additive equivalence of $G$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{Injective} (\operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{ofInjective} (\mathrm{hf})\in \mathrm{AddEquiv} (G , \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})})$
French
Supposons que $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$ est injectif. Alors l'homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $\mathrm{hf}$ est une équivalence additive de $G$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.
AddMonoidHom.ofLeftInversedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker, function or constant
Wording
the additive monoid homomorphism of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $\operatorname{LeftInverse}$ holds for $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $g$ and $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the additive monoid homomorphism of $h$ is an additive equivalence of $G$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{LeftInverse} (\operatorname{coe} (g), \operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{ofLeftInverse} (h)\in \mathrm{AddEquiv} (G , \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})})$
French
Supposons que $\operatorname{LeftInverse}$ tient pour $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $g$ et $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors l'homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $h$ est une équivalence additive de $G$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{range} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.

AddMonoidHom.rangeRestrictdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subgroup.Ker, function or constant
Wording
the range restriction of f (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English

Mathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Basic

AddMonoidHom.ofClosureMEqTopLeftdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive monoid homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $M$ to elements of $N$. Assume that the closure of $s$ is equal to the top. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {f (0)}{0}$. Assume that for all elements $x_{2}$ of $M$, if $x_{2}$ is an element of $s$, then for all elements $y$ of $M$, $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Then the additive monoid homomorphism of $f$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and $N$.
Formula
$f \in M \rightarrow N , \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{closure} (s)}{\operatorname{top}}, \EqtMACRoJ {f (0)}{0}, \forall x_{2} \in M . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{x_{2}}\rightarrow (\forall y \in M . \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)})\vdash \operatorname{ofClosureMEqTopLeft} (f , \mathrm{hs} , h_{1} , \mathrm{hadd})\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (M , N)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $M$ à des éléments de $N$. Supposons que la clôture de $s$ est égale au top. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {f (0)}{0}$. Supposons que pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ de $M$, si $x_{2}$ est un élément de $s$, alors pour tous les éléments $y$ de $M$, $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Alors l'homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $M$ et $N$.
AddMonoidHom.ofClosureMEqTopRightdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Submonoid.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive monoid homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $M$ to elements of $N$. Assume that the closure of $s$ is equal to the top. Assume that $\EqtMACRoH {f (0)}{0}$. Assume that for all elements $x_{2}$ and $y$ of $M$, if $y$ is an element of $s$, then $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Then the additive monoid homomorphism of $f$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and $N$.
Formula
$f \in M \rightarrow N , \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{closure} (s)}{\operatorname{top}}, \EqtMACRoJ {f (0)}{0}, \forall x_{2} \in M . \forall y \in M . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{y}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}\vdash \operatorname{ofClosureMEqTopRight} (f , \mathrm{hs} , h_{1} , \mathrm{hadd})\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (M , N)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $M$ à des éléments de $N$. Supposons que la clôture de $s$ est égale au top. Supposons que $\EqtMACRoH {f (0)}{0}$. Supposons que pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ et $y$ de $M$, si $y$ est un élément de $s$, alors $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Alors l'homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $f$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $M$ et $N$.

English

AddHom.ofDensedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the additive homomorphism of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $M$ to elements of $N$. Assume that the closure of $s$ is equal to the top. Assume that for all elements $x_{2}$ and $y$ of $M$, if $y$ is an element of $s$, then $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. Then the additive homomorphism of $f$ is an additive homomorphism of $M$ and $N$.
Formula
$f \in M \rightarrow N , \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{closure} (s)}{\operatorname{top}}, \forall x_{2} \in M . \forall y \in M . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {s}{y}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}\vdash \operatorname{ofDense} (f , \mathrm{hs} , \mathrm{hadd})\in \mathrm{AddHom} (M , N)$
French

Supposons que pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ et $y$ de $M$, si $y$ est un élément de $s$, alors $\EqtMACRoH {f (x_{2} + y)}{f (x_{2})+ f (y)}$. the copy of S and s (from the name)
English
Let $S$ be a subsemigroup of $M$. Let $s$ be an element of $\wp M$ such that $\EqtMACRoH {s}{S}$. Then the copy of $S$ and $s$ is a subsemigroup of $M$.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Subsemigroup} (M), s \in \wp M , \EqtMACRoJ {s}{S}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (S , s , \mathrm{hs})\in \mathrm{Subsemigroup} (M)$
French
Soit $S$ un sous-demi-groupe de $M$. Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp M$ tel que $\EqtMACRoH {s}{S}$. Alors le copy de $S$ et $s$ est un sous-demi-groupe de $M$.
Subsemigroup.ofClassdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the subsemigroup of s (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an element of $S$. Then the subsemigroup of $s$ is a subsemigroup of $M$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $s$ and the function that maps $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$ to $\mathrm{MulMemClass\_\_mul\_mem}$.
Formula
$s \in S \vdash \operatorname{ofClass} (s):= \operatorname{mk} (s , \lambda x_{2} , x_{3} . \mathrm{MulMemClass\_\_mul\_mem})\in \mathrm{Subsemigroup} (M)$
French
Soit $s$ un élément de $S$. Alors le sous-demi-groupe de $s$ est un sous-demi-groupe de $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $s$ et la fonction qui envoie $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$ à $\mathrm{MulMemClass\_\_mul\_mem}$.

AddSubsemigroup.equivOpdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.MulOpposite, function or constant
Wording
the equivalence opposite of H (from the name)
English
Let $H$ be an additive subsemigroup of $M$. Then the equivalence opposite of $H$ is an element of $\EquivMACRoAE {(\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})})}{(\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{op} (H)}{x_{2}})})}$.
Formula
$H \in \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (M)\vdash \operatorname{equivOp} (H)\in \EquivMACRoAL {(\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {H}{x_{1}})})}{(\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{op} (H)}{x_{2}})})}$
French
Soit $H$ un sous-demi-groupe additif de $M$. Alors le opposite d'équivalences de $H$ est un élément de $\EquivMACRoAE {(\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {H}{x_{1}})})}{(\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{op} (H)}{x_{2}})})}$.
AddSubsemigroup.opdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.MulOpposite, function or constant
Wording
the opposite of x1 (from the name)
English

Formula
$x_{1} \in \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (M)\vdash \operatorname{op} (x_{1})\in \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (\mathrm{AddOpposite} (M))$

AddSubsemigroup.opEquivdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.MulOpposite, function or constant
Wording
the opposite equivalence (from the name)
English
The opposite equivalence is an order isomorphism of the set of additive subsemigroups of $M$ and the set of additive subsemigroups of the set of additive opposites of $M$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\operatorname{op}$, $\operatorname{unop}$, $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_unop\_op}$ and $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_unop}$ and the function that maps $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ to $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_le\_op\_iff}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{opEquiv} := \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{op} , \operatorname{unop} , \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_unop\_op} , \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_unop}), \lambda x_{1} , x_{2} . \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_le\_op\_iff})\in \mathrm{OrderIso} (\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (M), \mathrm{AddSubsemigroup} (\mathrm{AddOpposite} (M)))$
French
L'équivalence opposée est un isomorphisme d'ordres de l'ensemble de sous-demi-groupes additifs de $M$ et l'ensemble de sous-demi-groupes additifs de l'ensemble de opposite additifs de $M$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{op}$, $\operatorname{unop}$, $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_unop\_op}$ et $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_unop}$ et la fonction qui envoie $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ à $\mathrm{AddSubsemigroup\_\_op\_le\_op\_iff}$.

AddEquiv.ofLeftInversedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Subsemigroup.Operations, function or constant
Wording
the additive equivalence of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an additive homomorphism of $M$ and $N$ such that $\operatorname{LeftInverse}$ holds for $g$ and $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the additive equivalence of $f$ and $h$ is an additive equivalence of $M$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{srange} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddHom} (M , N), \operatorname{LeftInverse} (g , \operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{ofLeftInverse} (f , h)\in \mathrm{AddEquiv} (M , \SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {\operatorname{srange} (f)}{x_{2}})})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme additif de $M$ et $N$ tel que $\operatorname{LeftInverse}$ tienne pour $g$ et $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors l'équivalence additive de $f$ et $h$ est une équivalence additive de $M$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {\operatorname{srange} (f)}{x_{2}})}$.

Wording
the translation of f and x2 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $a$ and $x_{2}$ be elements of $G$. Let $f$ be a function from elements of $G$ to elements of $x$. Then the translation of $f$ and $x_{2}$ is an element of $x$ defined as $f$ applied to $x_{2} - a$.
Formula
$a \in G , f \in G \rightarrow x , x_{2} \in G \vdash \mathrm{translate} (a , f , x_{2}):= f (x_{2} - a)\in x$
French
Soient $a$ et $x_{2}$ des éléments de $G$. Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $G$ à des éléments de $x$. Alors la translation de $f$ et $x_{2}$ est un élément de $x$ défini comme $f$ appliqué à $x_{2} - a$.

AdditivedefMathlib.Algebra.Group.TypeTags.Basic, type
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then an element of $\mathrm{Additive}$ applied to $x$ is an element of the function that maps $x$ to $x$.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{Additive} (x):= \lambda x . x$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors un élément de $\mathrm{Additive}$ appliqué à $x$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $x$ à $x$.

UniqueAdddefMathlib.Algebra.Group.UniqueProds.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $A$ and $B$ be finite sets of $G$. Let $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$ be elements of $G$. Then $\mathrm{UniqueAdd}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$, if for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $G$, if $a$ is an element of $A$, then if $b$ is an element of $B$, then if $\EqtMACRoH {a + b}{a_{0} + b_{0}}$, then $\EqtMACRoH {a}{a_{0}}$ and $\EqtMACRoH {b}{b_{0}}$.
Formula
$A , B \in \mathrm{Finset} (G), a_{0} , b_{0} \in G \vdash \mathrm{UniqueAdd} (A , B , a_{0} , b_{0}):\Leftrightarrow \forall a \in G . \forall b \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {A}{a}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoAA {B}{b}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {a + b}{a_{0} + b_{0}}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {a}{a_{0}}\wedge (\EqtMACRoJ {b}{b_{0}})$
French
Soient $A$ et $B$ des ensembles finis de $G$. Soient $a_{0}$ et $b_{0}$ des éléments de $G$. Alors $\mathrm{UniqueAdd}$ tient pour $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ et $b_{0}$ si pour tous les éléments $a$ et $b$ de $G$, si $a$ est un élément de $A$, alors si $b$ est un élément de $B$, alors si $\EqtMACRoH {a + b}{a_{0} + b_{0}}$, alors $\EqtMACRoH {a}{a_{0}}$ et $\EqtMACRoH {b}{b_{0}}$.
UniqueMuldefMathlib.Algebra.Group.UniqueProds.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $A$ and $B$ be finite sets of $G$. Let $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$ be elements of $G$. Then $\mathrm{UniqueMul}$ holds for $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ and $b_{0}$, if for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $G$, if $a$ is an element of $A$, then if $b$ is an element of $B$, then if $\EqtMACRoH {a \times b}{a_{0} \times b_{0}}$, then $\EqtMACRoH {a}{a_{0}}$ and $\EqtMACRoH {b}{b_{0}}$.
Formula
$A , B \in \mathrm{Finset} (G), a_{0} , b_{0} \in G \vdash \mathrm{UniqueMul} (A , B , a_{0} , b_{0}):\Leftrightarrow \forall a \in G . \forall b \in G . \MembershipmemMACRoAA {A}{a}\rightarrow \MembershipmemMACRoAA {B}{b}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {a \times b}{a_{0} \times b_{0}}\rightarrow \EqtMACRoJ {a}{a_{0}}\wedge (\EqtMACRoJ {b}{b_{0}})$

Alors $\mathrm{UniqueMul}$ tient pour $A$, $B$, $a_{0}$ et $b_{0}$ si pour tous les éléments $a$ et $b$ de $G$, si $a$ est un élément de $A$, alors si $b$ est un élément de $B$, alors si $\EqtMACRoH {a \times b}{a_{0} \times b_{0}}$, alors $\EqtMACRoH {a}{a_{0}}$ et $\EqtMACRoH {b}{b_{0}}$.

AddUnitsstructureMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, type
Wording
additive unit of x (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be a set. Then the additive units of $x$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $x$ un ensemble. Alors les unités additives de $x$ sont un type de base.
AddUnits.copydefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the copy of val and neg (from the name)
English
Let $u$ be an additive unit of $x$. Let $\mathrm{val}$ and $\mathrm{neg}$ be elements of $x$ such that $\mathrm{val}$ is equal to the underlying value in the base of $u$ and $\mathrm{neg}$ is equal to the underlying value in the base of $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $u$. Then the copy of $\mathrm{val}$ and $\mathrm{neg}$ is an additive unit of $x$.
Formula
$u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x), \mathrm{val} \in x , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{val}}{\operatorname{val} (u)}, \mathrm{neg} \in x , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{neg}}{\operatorname{val} (\operatorname{neg} (u))}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (u , \mathrm{val} , \mathrm{hv} , \mathrm{neg} , \mathrm{hi})\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)$
French
Soit $u$ une unité additive de $x$. Soient $\mathrm{val}$ et $\mathrm{neg}$ des éléments de $x$ tels que $\mathrm{val}$ soit égal au [DepNoun] sous-jacent de $u$ et $\mathrm{neg}$ soit égal au [DepNoun] sous-jacent de $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $u$. Alors le copy de $\mathrm{val}$ et $\mathrm{neg}$ est une unité additive de $x$.

no wording

IsAddUnitdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, predicate
Wording
a is a additive unit (from the name)
English
Let $a$ be an element of $M$. Then $a$ is an additive unit, if there exists an additive unit $u$ of $M$, such that the underlying value in the base of $u$ is equal to $a$.
Formula
$a \in M \vdash \mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a):\Leftrightarrow \exists u \in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{val} (u)}{a}$
French
Soit $a$ un élément de $M$. Alors $a$ est une unité additive s'il existe une unité additive $u$ de $M$ tel que le [DepNoun] sous-jacent de $u$ soit égal à $a$.
IsAddUnit.addUnitdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive unit of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $a$ is an additive unit. Then the additive unit of $h$ is an additive unit of $M$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a)\vdash \operatorname{addUnit} (h)\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (M)$
French
Supposons que $a$ est une unité additive. Alors l'unité additive de $h$ est une unité additive de $M$.
IsAddUnit.addUnit'defMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant
Wording
the additive unit of h (from the name)
English
Assume that $a$ is an additive unit. Then the additive unit of $h$ is an additive unit of $x$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $a$, $\operatorname{neg}$ applied to $a$, $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel}$ applied to $h$ and $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel}$ applied to $h$.
Formula
$\mathrm{IsAddUnit} (a)\vdash \operatorname{addUnit'} (h):= \operatorname{mk} (a , \operatorname{neg} (a), \mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel} (h), \mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel} (h))\in \mathrm{AddUnits} (x)$
French
Supposons que $a$ est une unité additive. Alors l'unité additive de $h$ est une unité additive de $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $a$, $\operatorname{neg}$ appliqué à $a$, $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_add\_neg\_cancel}$ appliqué à $h$ et $\mathrm{IsAddUnit\_\_neg\_add\_cancel}$ appliqué à $h$.

IsUnit.unit'defMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Defs, function or constant

AddUnits.liftRightdefMathlib.Algebra.Group.Units.Hom, function or constant
Wording
the right lift of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and $N$. Let $g$ be a function from elements of $M$ to additive units of $N$ such that for all elements $x_{2}$ of $M$, the underlying value in the base of $g$ applied to $x_{2}$ is equal to $f$ applied to $x_{2}$. Then the right lift of $f$ and $g$ is an additive monoid homomorphism of $M$ and the set of additive units of $N$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (M , N), g \in M \rightarrow \mathrm{AddUnits} (N), \forall x_{2} \in M . \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{val} (g (x_{2}))}{f (x_{2})}\vdash \operatorname{liftRight} (f , g , h)\in \mathrm{AddMonoidHom} (M , \mathrm{AddUnits} (N))$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $M$ et $N$. Soit $g$ une fonction des éléments de $M$ à des unités additives de $N$ telle que pour tous les éléments $x_{2}$ de $M$, le [DepNoun] sous-jacent de $g$ appliqué à $x_{2}$ soit égal à $f$ appliqué à $x_{2}$. Alors le lift droit de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes additif de $M$ et l'ensemble d'unités additives de $N$.
